    var lang = getLang();

    function readCookie(name) {
        return (name = new RegExp('(?:^|;\\s*)' + ('' + name).replace(/[-[\]{}()*+?.,\\^$|#\s]/g, '\\$&') + '=([^;]*)').exec(document.cookie)) && name[1];
    }
    if (readCookie('apex__dfc_locale') === null) {
        document.cookie = 'apex__dfc_locale=' + lang.code + ';path=/;';
        window.location.reload()
    }
    // check if we need this function
    if (lang.code !== readCookie('apex__dfc_locale')) {
        document.cookie = 'apex__dfc_locale=' + lang.code + ';path=/;';
    }

<script>
    function call_campaigns_cookie(){
        var params = document.location.search.substring(1);
        var utm_source = '';
        var utm_medium = '';
        var utm_campaign = '';
        var dr = '';
        params_array = params.split('&');
        for(i=0;i<params_array.length;i++){
            param_array = params_array[i].split('=');
            if(param_array[0]=='utm_source'){
                utm_source = param_array[1];
            }else if(param_array[0]=='utm_medium'){
                utm_medium = param_array[1];
            }else if(param_array[0]=='utm_campaign'){
                utm_campaign = param_array[1];
            }else if(param_array[0]=='dr'){
                dr = param_array[1];
            }
        }
  
        if(utm_source!=null && utm_source!='' &&
            utm_medium!=null && utm_medium!='' &&
            utm_campaign!=null && utm_campaign!=''){
            //do call
            $.get( "/events/campaigns_cookie", { utm_source: utm_source, utm_medium: utm_medium, utm_campaign: utm_campaign, dr: dr } );
        }
    }
    function animateSearchIn() {
        $('#mobileSearchContainerOverlay').fadeIn();
        $('#mobileSearchContainer').fadeIn();
        $('#st-search-input-mobile').focus();
        $("#mobileSearchContainer .searchBar").animate({
          top: '0px',
        }, 500, function() {
  
            $("#mobileSearchContainer .searchBar").animate({
              top: '-20px',
            }, 300);
  
        });
    }
  
    function animateSearchOut() {
        $("#mobileSearchContainer .searchBar").animate({
          top: '0px',
        }, 300, function() {
            $("#mobileSearchContainer .searchBar").animate({
              top: '-80px',
            }, 500, function() {
                $('#mobileSearchContainer').fadeOut();
                $('#mobileSearchContainerOverlay').fadeOut();
                $('#st-search-input-mobile').val('');
            });
        });
    }
    function swiftypeSearch(){
      $('.search-form').submit(function(event){
        event.preventDefault();
        var query = encodeURIComponent($(this).find('input').val());
        animateSearchOut();
        //window.location = '/stype-search?stq=' + query;
        
        window.location = 'https://go.pardot.com/s/27572/bZmGraVP6uMckd2zJnklMehlfDezI7wZ?/%23stq=' + query;
      });
  $('.header-st-search-button').click(function(){
  $('.search-form').submit();
  });
  $('#mobileSearch').unbind('click');
  
  $('#mobileSearch').click(function(){
  animateSearchIn();
  });
  $('#mobileSearchContainerOverlay, .clear-button').click(function(){
  animateSearchOut();
  });
  $('#st-search-input-mobile').after($("<button type='submit' class='header-st-search-button-left'></button>"));
  
  }
  
    function mobileSidebarFix(){
        if( $('body').width() <= 979 ){
          $('.mobileHeaderContainer').addClass('on-mobile');
        }else{
          $('.mobileHeaderContainer').removeClass('on-mobile');
        }
    }
  
    var hideMobileHeaderContainer = true;
  
  
    $(document).ready(function(){
  
        /*!
        * jquery.scrollto.js 0.0.1 - https://github.com/yckart/jquery.scrollto.js
        * Scroll smooth to any element in your DOM.
        *
        * Copyright (c) 2012 Yannick Albert (http://yckart.com)
        * Licensed under the MIT license (http://www.opensource.org/licenses/mit-license.php).
        * 2013/02/17
        **/
        $.scrollTo = $.fn.scrollTo = function(x, y, options){
            if (!(this instanceof $)) return $.fn.scrollTo.apply($('html, body'), arguments);
  
            options = $.extend({}, {
                gap: {
                    x: 0,
                    y: 1
                },
                animation: {
                    easing: 'swing',
                    duration: 600,
                    complete: $.noop,
                    step: $.noop
                }
            }, options);
  
            return this.each(function(){
                var elem = $(this);
                elem.stop().animate({
                    scrollLeft: !isNaN(Number(x)) ? x : $(y).offset().left + options.gap.x,
                    scrollTop: !isNaN(Number(y)) ? y : $(y).offset().top + options.gap.y
                }, options.animation);
            });
        };
                                                                                                                                          swiftypeSearch();
        mobileSidebarFix();
  
  
        $(window).scroll(function() {
  
            if( hideMobileHeaderContainer == true && $(window).scrollLeft() > ($('.mobileHeaderContainer').outerWidth()/2) ){
                hideMobileHeaderContainer = false;
  
                setTimeout(function(){hideMobileHeaderContainer=true;}, 600);
                //$('html,body').scrollTo(0, 0, function(){});
                $('header').removeClass('showMobile');
                $('#SDNmaintemplate').removeClass('showMobile');
                $('footer').removeClass('showMobile');
            }
        });
  
        $(window).resize(function(){
          mobileSidebarFix();
        });
        call_campaigns_cookie();
    });
  
  </script>
<link href="https://developer.salesforce.com/resources2/stylesheets/dsc-header.css" rel="stylesheet" type="text/css"/>

<h2 class="slds-text-heading--medium slds-m-bottom--medium">
					Be a <span class="Ff(trailhead)">Trailblazer</span> - Deliver continuous innovation.
				</h2>
<p>
					The Salesforce Platform connects the entire customer journey across Sales, Service, Marketing, Commerce, and every touchpoint. Trailblazers like you deliver continuous innovation using powerful tools built right into one customer success platform, including modern app development practices, analytics, AI, and secure data integration. <a href="https://developer.salesforce.com/platform" target="_blank">Learn more</a>.
				</p>

<script>
    
var isMobile = {
    Android: function() {
        return navigator.userAgent.match(/Android/i);
    },
    BlackBerry: function() {
        return navigator.userAgent.match(/BlackBerry/i);
    },
    iOS: function() {
        return navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone|iPad|iPod/i);
    },
    Opera: function() {
        return navigator.userAgent.match(/Opera Mini/i);
    },
    Windows: function() {
        return navigator.userAgent.match(/IEMobile/i);
    },
    iPad: function(){
    	return navigator.userAgent.match(/iPad/i);
    },
    any: function() {
        return (isMobile.Android() || isMobile.BlackBerry() || isMobile.iOS() || isMobile.Opera() || isMobile.Windows());
    }
};

    
function makeTopSlider(){
	if( (isMobile.any() && !isMobile.iPad()) || $('body').width() <= 979 ){
		$("#full-width-slider2").royalSlider({
            arrowsNav:0,
            keyboardNavEnabled:0,
            controlsInside:0,
            imageScaleMode:"fill",
            arrowsNavAutoHide:1,
            autoScaleSlider:0,
            autoScaleSliderWidth:960,
            autoScaleSliderHeight:325,
            controlNavigation:"tabs",
            thumbs:{},
            thumbsFitInViewport:1,
            navigateByClick:0,
            startSlideId:0,
            autoPlay:0,
            transitionType:"move",
            globalCaption:0,
            loop:1,
            numImagesToPreload:4,
            deeplinking:{enabled:0,change:1},
            imgWidth:1024,
            imgHeight:325,
            slidesOrientation: 'horizontal',
            sliderDrag:true,
            sliderTouch: false
        });
    }else{
        $("#full-width-slider2").royalSlider({
            arrowsNav:0,
            keyboardNavEnabled:0,
            controlsInside:0,
            imageScaleMode:"fill",
            arrowsNavAutoHide:1,
            autoScaleSlider:0,
            autoScaleSliderWidth:960,
            autoScaleSliderHeight:325,
            controlNavigation:"tabs",
            thumbs:{},
            thumbsFitInViewport:1,
            navigateByClick:0,
            startSlideId:0,
            autoPlay:0,
            transitionType:"move",
            globalCaption:0,
            loop:1,
            numImagesToPreload:4,
            deeplinking:{enabled:0,change:1},
            imgWidth:1024,
            imgHeight:325,
            slidesOrientation: 'vertical',
            sliderDrag:false,
            sliderTouch: false
        });
    }  
}
    
makeTopSlider();
    
$(document).ready(function(){
    $('#sliderMenu .span-menu').append($('#full-width-slider2 .rsTabs'));
    
    if( !isMobile.any() ){
        $('#full-width-slider2 .rsSlide').each(function(){
          var $slide = $(this);
          var link = $('a.btn', $slide).attr('href');

          $('.span4 .logo-in-slider', $slide).click(function(){
            location.href = link;
          });

          $('.span4 .logo-in-slider', $slide).css({
            cursor: 'pointer'
          });
        });
    }
    
    $('.logo-in-slider').each(function(){
        if( $(this).attr('src') == null || $(this).attr('src') == '' ){
        	$(this).hide();
        }
    });
    
    $( window ).resize(function(){
    	makeTopSlider();
    });
});
</script> </div></div></div>
